# v46 来源清单（99）

> 本文件汇总 `_arch_v46/` 全部 100 个方向文件「## 来源」节引用的 URL。
> 抽取脚本口径：匹配每个方向文件「## 来源」标题之后的所有 `http(s)://` 链接。
> 总计引用链接 **1779** 条（去重前）；全 100 文件正文内出现的**唯一 URL 1715 条**（跨 708 个域名）。
> ⚠️ 二手源提示：`zhuanlan.zhihu.com` / `blog.csdn.net` / `www.sohu.com` / `www.toutiao.com` / `www.nowcoder.com` 等中文自媒体/论坛占比不低，**引用前须回溯一手来源**（各方向「盲区」节已逐条标注）。

### 05_NeurIPS_ED审稿标准详解
1. 2024 Reviewer Guidelines — NeurIPS — https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2024/ReviewerGuidelines — 11 个表单问题；Soundness/Presentation/Contribution 各 1–4；Overall 1–10（含 10 档完整定义）；Confidence 1–5 — NeurIPS，2024
2. 2025 Reviewer Guidelines — NeurIPS — https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2025/ReviewerGuidelines — 13 个表单问题；Quality/Clarity/Significance/Originality 各 1–4；Overall 1–6（含 6 档完整定义）；Confidence 1–5；"state the clear criteria under which your evaluation score could increase or decrease" — NeurIPS，2025
3. NeurIPS Evaluations & Datasets 2026 Reviewing Guidelines — https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2026/EvaluationsDatasetsReviewerGuidelines — 四维度定义；8 类贡献的强制检查项；"reviewers may not use any LLMs or AI agents in the review process" — NeurIPS，2026-06-25
4. Reflecting on the 2025 Review Process from the Datasets and Benchmarks Chairs — https://blog.neurips.cc/2025/09/30/reflecting-on-the-2025-review-process-from-the-datasets-and-benchmarks-chairs/ — "skew higher and have a tighter distribution" / "track-wide average score of 4.25" / relative ranking 机制 — NeurIPS Blog，2025-09-30
5. Reflections on the 2025 Review Process from the Program Committee Chairs — https://blog.neurips.cc/2025/09/30/reflections-on-the-2025-review-process-from-the-program-committee-chairs/ — "reject decisions even when papers had high ratings" / "reviewers increasing their scores just to end back-and-forth discussion" / "11 unfortunate cases" — NeurIPS Blog，2025-09-30
6. Paper Copilot 原始数据（分数分布复算来源）— https://github.com/papercopilot/paperlists — `nips/nips2024.json`（31,753,965 字节，D&B 547 条，526 条有 rating）、`nips/nips2025.json`（38,038,097 字节，D&B 591 条）— Paper Copilot，2026 访问

### 06_NeurIPS_rebuttal翻盘真实案例
1. Kargaran, A. H., Nikeghbal, N., Yang, J., Ousidhoum, N. (2025). *Insights from the ICLR Peer Review and Rebuttal Process.* arXiv:2511.15462. https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15462 ；数据与代码：https://github.com/papercopilot/iclr-insights

4. Li, L., Aldarmaki, I., Fu, M. 等 (2025). *How Effective is Your Rebuttal? Identifying Causal Models from the OpenReview System.* ICLR 2026 Withdrawn Submission，Submission Number 9704。https://openreview.net/forum?id=tysOWd3RWm

6. NeurIPS (2025). *NeurIPS 2025 FAQ for Authors*（per-review rebuttal 10,000 字符上限、禁止链接、rebuttal 结束时才可见）。https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2025/PaperInformation/NeurIPS-FAQ
7. NeurIPS Communications Chairs (2025-09-30). *Reflections on the 2025 Review Process from the Program Committee Chairs*（21,575 / 5,290 / 24.52% / 20,518 reviewers / 11 例 desk reject）。https://blog.neurips.cc/2025/09/30/reflections-on-the-2025-review-process-from-the-program-committee-chairs/

9. 知乎 (2026-07-25). 《从 OpenReview 上的低分转高分论文看如何 rebuttal》。https://zhuanlan.zhihu.com/p/2064466275745653337
10. 知乎 (2024-11-28). 《rebuttal 真的有用！这篇 ICLR 论文，所有审稿人都加了 2 分》（SANA 案例）。https://zhuanlan.zhihu.com/p/9533078343

### 07_NeurIPS_desk_reject常见原因
1. NeurIPS (2026). *Main Track Handbook 2026*（9 页 / 50MB / 格式 / 匿名 / 双投 / 作者档案 / 幻觉引用 / LLM / 伦理 / arXiv 条款原文）。https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2026/MainTrackHandbook
2. NeurIPS (2026). *Call For Papers 2026*（track 不可互投、"Irrelevant or duplicate papers risk desk rejection from all tracks"）。https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2026/CallForPapers
3. NeurIPS (2026). *Call For Evaluations & Datasets 2026*（Croissant 强制、code policy、accessibility desk reject 原文）。https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2026/CallForEvaluationsDatasets
4. NeurIPS (2026). *Evaluations and Datasets 2026 Reviewing Guidelines*（LLM 严格禁令、desk reject 情形清单、reviewer 制裁条款）。https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2026/EvaluationsDatasetsReviewerGuidelines
5. NeurIPS. *Paper Checklist Guidelines*（16 条逐字条款、"papers not including the checklist will be desk rejected"、PDF 顺序）。https://neurips.cc/public/guides/PaperChecklist
6. NeurIPS (2025). *Reviewer Guidelines 2025*（"Desk Rejection Sanction: Grossly negligent reviews can result in desk rejection of reviewer's own papers"）。https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2025/ReviewerGuidelines
7. NeurIPS Communications Chairs (2025-09-30). *Reflections on the 2025 Review Process from the Program Committee Chairs*（21,575 / 5,290 / 24.52%；11 例 co-author desk reject）。https://blog.neurips.cc/2025/09/30/reflections-on-the-2025-review-process-from-the-program-committee-chairs/
8. CASRAI Editorial Board (2026-07-23). *NeurIPS 2026: Pangram AI-Detector Desk Rejections*（969–971 / 178 / 18.4% / 123 / 12.7% / 42.7% / 273 / 100 词窗口）。https://casrai.org/news/neurips-2026-pangram-ai-detector-desk-rejection-controversy

3. NeurIPS 2026 Submission Guide（serre-ai 整理；9 页正文、16 问 checklist、desk-reject 触发条件、contribution type 五选一）— https://github.com/serre-ai/research/blob/main/docs/submission-guides/neurips-2026.md
4. NeurIPS Main Track Handbook 2026（9 页、50MB、附录独立、contribution type 定义）— https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2026/MainTrackHandbook
5. NeurIPS Datasets & Benchmarks Track: From Art to Science in AI Evaluations — NeurIPS Blog，2025-12-05（Croissant 首轮缺字段 11.9%/4.9%/3.5%；851 作者 + 155 评审问卷；25% 作者认为评审质量待改进）— https://blog.neurips.cc/2025/12/05/neurips-datasets-benchmarks-track-from-art-to-science-in-ai-evaluations/
6. 12 Weeks to a Published Paper: The Exact Timeline Top PhD Students Follow — ScholarDue，2025-11-28，Jin Park（Weeks 1–2 文献 / 3–4 方法 / 5–6 实验 / 7–8 写稿 / 9–10 修改 / 11–12 润色提交；「submit at least 24 hours before the deadline」）— https://scholardue.com/en/blog/paper-writing-timeline
7. How to Write a Great Research Paper — Simon Peyton Jones（七条建议；「write the paper as you go」的原始论述见其 slides PDF）— https://simon.peytonjones.org/great-research-paper/ ；slides：https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/research/uploads/prod/2016/07/How-to-write-a-great-research-paper.pdf
8. NeurIPS Newsletter – July 2026（rebuttal 时间边界澄清：07-27~08-03 AoE 为作者回应期）— https://blog.neurips.cc/2026/07/31/neurips-newsletter-july-2026/
9. NeurIPS 2026 Rebuttal and Discussion Timeline Clarified — NeurIPS 官方 LinkedIn 帖，2026-07-28 — https://www.linkedin.com/posts/nipsconference_a-clarification-on-the-timeframe-for-rebuttals-activity-7487757130478034944-TuR6
10. Clopper-Pearson confidence interval for one-sample proportion — lrstat R 包文档 — https://search.r-project.org/CRAN/refmans/lrstat/html/ClopperPearsonCI.html
11. Ultimate Guide to Clopper-Pearson Intervals — Number Analytics，2025-05-18（小样本二项比例的精确区间，Wald 区间在小 n 下失效）— https://www.numberanalytics.com/blog/clopper-pearson-interval-guide
12. ICML 2027 / NeurIPS 2027 社区 deadline 估值 — https://www.opencurious.com/ai-conference-deadlines/icml-2027 、https://researchtheta.com/conferences/neurips-2027/ 、https://www.opencurious.com/ai-conference-deadlines/neurips-2027
13. 仓库锚点（本方向数字来源）— `_arch_v46/00_仓库扫描.md`：holdout 30（真错 17/对照 9/unknown 4）、corpus 40（A 54.2%/B 12.5%/C 0%）、fixtures 15、规则 67（block 44/warn 16/advice 7）、`gate_engine.py` 3826 行、`_auto/status.json` active_batch=660 vs 实际 665
14. 论文 v0.3 关键数字（`research/paper_v0.3.md`，117 行，2026-09-29，665 批）— reveal_3 检出率 66.7%（10/15 可测）、外部 corpus 43.8%、-O1→-O0 使 3/5 miss 被同一 sanitizer 抓住、变异测试 97.3%/81.5% 被主动放弃

### 18_Abstract写作模板
1. SWE-bench: Can Language Models Resolve Real-world Github Issues? — Carlos E Jimenez, John Yang, Alexander Wettig, Shunyu Yao, Kexin Pei, Ofir Press, Karthik Narasimhan，ICLR 2024（Abstract 逐字）— https://proceedings.iclr.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/edac78c3e300629acfe6cbe9ca88fb84-Abstract.html
2. GAIA: a benchmark for General AI Assistants — Grégoire Mialon, Clémentine Fourrier, Craig Swift, Thomas Wolf, Yann LeCun, Thomas Scialom，arXiv:2311.12983（2023-11-21 提交）— https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.12983
3. DataComp: In search of the next generation of multimodal datasets — Samir Yitzhak Gadre 等 33 人，NeurIPS 2023 Datasets and Benchmarks Track（Abstract 逐字）— https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2023/hash/56332d41d55ad7ad8024aac625881be7-Abstract-Datasets_and_Benchmarks.html
4. AgentBench: Evaluating LLMs as Agents — Xiao Liu, Hao Yu, Hanchen Zhang 等 22 人，ICLR 2024；arXiv:2308.03688v3（2025-10-04 修订）— https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.03688
5. Finding and Understanding Bugs in C Compilers — Xuejun Yang, Yang Chen, Eric Eide, John Regehr，PLDI 2011（Award paper），DOI 10.1145/1993498.1993532（Abstract 逐字）— https://www.flux.utah.edu/paper/yang-pldi11
6. How to Write an Abstract: Structure, Length, and Examples — Texio Academy，2026-06-02（五句式模型、弱句 vs 强句对照、七步字数压缩法、长度表：短篇 100–150 / 学期论文 150–200 / 研究论文 200–250 / 期刊式 250–300）— https://texio.academy/en/articles/how-to-write-abstract
7. 12 Weeks to a Published Paper: The Exact Timeline Top PhD Students Follow — ScholarDue，2025-11-28（「Write the abstract last — it should summarize, not preview」）— https://scholardue.com/en/blog/paper-writing-timeline
8. NeurIPS 2026 Page Limit — TypeTeX（「The abstract is one paragraph, typically 150–250 words. It must be self-contained (no citations, no math you haven't introduced)」）— https://www.typetex.app/templates/neurips/page-limit
9. NeurIPS Datasets & Benchmarks Track: From Art to Science in AI Evaluations — NeurIPS Blog，2025-12-05（851 作者 + 155 评审问卷；25% 作者认为评审质量待改进，抱怨含「emphasis on methodological novelty over real-world impact」）— https://blog.neurips.cc/2025/12/05/neurips-datasets-benchmarks-track-from-art-to-science-in-ai-evaluations/
10. 仓库锚点 — `_arch_v46/00_仓库扫描.md`（holdout 30 / 真错 17、corpus 40、fixtures 15、规则 67（block 44）、`gate_engine.py` 3826 行、重注入 6/6）与 `research/paper_v0.3.md`（66.7% / 43.8% / P=R=F1=0）
11. NeurIPS Paper Checklist Guidelines — https://neurips.cc/public/guides/PaperChecklist （16 问清单，其中第 1 问即「Claims: Abstract/intro match actual contributions and scope」——直接约束 Abstract 不得 overclaim）

### 19_贡献声明怎么写
1. NeurIPS Paper Checklist Guidelines（16 问；第 1 问 Claims、第 2 问 Limitations 且「reviewers told not to penalize」、第 7 问 error bar 说明要求）— https://neurips.cc/public/guides/PaperChecklist

3. NeurIPS Evaluations & Datasets 2026 Reviewing Guidelines（Quality 维度问「Are the authors careful and honest about evaluating both the strengths and weaknesses」；LLM 政策与披露要求）— https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2026/EvaluationsDatasetsReviewerGuidelines
4. DataComp: In search of the next generation of multimodal datasets — Samir Yitzhak Gadre 等 33 人，NeurIPS 2023 D&B；贡献原文见 arXiv:2304.14108v5 第 1 节末（第四、第五贡献；6.9pp / 1.2pp / 79.2% / 9× / 3.7pp / Table 1 / Table 3 / Table 18）— https://arxiv.org/html/2304.14108v5
5. Feature Coding in the Era of Large Models: Dataset, Test Conditions, and Benchmark — Changsheng Gao, Yifan Ma, Qiaoxi Chen, Yenan Xu, Dong Liu, Weisi Lin，arXiv:2412.04307（「make three fundamental contributions. First, ... Second, ... Third, ...」逐字）— https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2412.04307
6. HybridCodeAuthorship: A Benchmark Dataset for Line-Level Code Authorship Detection — arXiv:2606.12620（「Our contribution is two-fold: 1. ... 2. ...」逐字；AIGCode Detector F1 0.48 / 0.56）— https://arxiv.org/html/2606.12620
7. SWE-bench: Can Language Models Resolve Real-world Github Issues? — Carlos E Jimenez, John Yang, Alexander Wettig, Shunyu Yao, Kexin Pei, Ofir Press, Karthik Narasimhan，ICLR 2024（2,294 / 12 仓库 / 19,000 训练实例 / 37 仓库 / 7b·13b / >100,000 tokens / 1.96%）— https://proceedings.iclr.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/edac78c3e300629acfe6cbe9ca88fb84-Abstract.html

9. NeurIPS Datasets & Benchmarks Track: From Art to Science in AI Evaluations — NeurIPS Blog，2025-12-05（851 作者 + 155 评审问卷；25% 作者认为评审质量待改进；抱怨含「emphasis on methodological novelty over real-world impact」）— https://blog.neurips.cc/2025/12/05/neurips-datasets-benchmarks-track-from-art-to-science-in-ai-evaluations/
10. TRAP: Hijacking VLA CoT-Reasoning via Adversarial Patches — OpenReview 公开评审（「For a paper making broad claims about reasoning-VLA security, this is still a fairly small and curated empirical base.」）— https://openreview.net/forum?id=zLUPTgV7BI
11. 审稿人视角：你的 IEEE 论文在 Related Work 里踩了哪些雷？— CSDN，2026-08-19（「读完 Related Work 后，我仍然不知道这些研究与本文有何实质关联」；批判性比较三维度表）— https://blog.csdn.net/weixin_28691741/article/details/160785988
12. 单一作者 SCI 论文贡献声明撰写指南 — SCI期刊网（单作者叙述式 CRediT 范式例句）— http://www.sciqk.com/lwgl/lwfbzn/16807.html
13. NeurIPS 2026 Main Track Handbook（Author Use of Agents and Large Language Models 政策；`\begin{ack}` 匿名期自动隐藏；contribution type 五选一）— https://neurips.cc/Conferences/2026/MainTrackHandbook
14. 仓库锚点与论文 v0.3 数字 — `_arch_v46/00_仓库扫描.md`（67 规则 / 44 block / `gate_engine.py` 3826 行 / holdout 30 / corpus 40 / fixtures 15 / 重注入 6/6 / `_auto/status.json` 漂移）与 `research/paper_v0.3.md`（66.7% / 43.8% / A 54.2% / B 12.5% / C 0% / P=R=F1=0 / -O1→-O0 使 3/5 miss 被同一 sanitizer 抓住）

SWE-bench: Can Language Models Resolve Real-World GitHub Issues? — Carlos E Jimenez, John Yang, Alexander Wettig, Shunyu Yao, Kexin Pei, Ofir Press, Karthik Narasimhan，ICLR 2024；Related Work 见第 6 节（3 段：Evaluation of LMs / Code Generation Benchmarks / ML for Software Engineering；点名 BIG-bench、HELM、WebShop、WebArena、Mind2Web、AgentBench、HumanEval、MultiPL-E、Defects4J、ManySStuBs4J）— https://ar5iv.labs.arxiv.org/html/2310.06770 （原文 PDF：https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.06770）

Dietterich, "Approximate Statistical Tests for Comparing Supervised Classification Learning Algorithms", *Neural Computation* **10(7): 1895–1923**, 1998（"The 5×2 cv test is shown to be slightly more powerful than McNemar's test."）— https://direct.mit.edu/neco/article/10/7/1895/6224/Approximate-Statistical-Tests-for-Comparing ；PDF: https://web.engr.oregonstate.edu/~tgd/publications/dietterich-approximate-statistical-tests-nc1998.pdf

### 29_内存错误检测工具对比
**官方文档（一手）**
1. Clang, AddressSanitizer — https://clang.llvm.org/docs/AddressSanitizer.html （"Typical slowdown ... 2x"、"up to 3x" stack、"16+ TB"、"not expected to produce false positives"、container overflow 误报、`-O1` 建议、LSan 平台支持）
2. Clang, ThreadSanitizer — https://bcain-llvm.readthedocs.io/projects/clang/en/latest/ThreadSanitizer/ ；https://hpc-wiki.info/hpc/ThreadSanitizer （"5x-15x" / "5x-10x" / "5x plus 1Mb per thread" / 3x、9x 档）
3. Clang, MemorySanitizer — https://clang.llvm.org/docs/MemorySanitizer.html （"3x"、"2x more real memory, 3x with origin tracking"、"64 TB"、origin tracking "1.5x-2x"、"all program code is instrumented"、"70+ interceptors"、平台仅 Linux/NetBSD/FreeBSD）
4. Clang, UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer — https://clang.llvm.org/docs/UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer.html （30+ 检查项完整清单、检查组定义、`object-size` 在高优化档更强、`add-signed-overflow-test` 不阻止 UB 优化、minimal runtime 不支持 `vptr`）
5. Clang, TypeSanitizer（严格别名） — https://clang.llvm.org/docs/TypeSanitizer.html
6. Valgrind, The Valgrind Quick Start Guide — https://valgrind.org/docs/manual/quick-start.html （"20 to 30 times"、"more than twice as much" memory、"typically right 99% of the time"、"can't detect out-of-range reads or writes to arrays that are allocated statically or on the stack"、"Use of -O2 and above is not recommended as Memcheck occasionally reports uninitialised-value errors which don't really exist"、leak 分类）
7. Microsoft Learn, 错误：alloc-dealloc-mismatch（Windows 默认关闭） — https://learn.microsoft.com/zh-cn/cpp/sanitizers/error-alloc-dealloc-mismatch?view=msvc-170
8. Android Developers, 调试和减少内存错误（HWASan / MTE 定位） — https://developer.android.google.cn/ndk/guides/memory-debug?hl=zh-cn
9. Huawei HarmonyOS 开发者文档, TSan（"5 到 15 倍" / "5 到 10 倍"、五 sanitizer 只能开一个） — https://developer.huawei.com/consumer/cn/doc/best-practices/bpta-stability-tsan-detection
10. Conan 文档, C/C++ 编译器 Sanitizers（ASan/TSan/MSan 互斥） — https://docs.conan.org.cn/2/security/sanitizers.html

**论文（一手）**
11. Serebryany, Bruening, Potapenko, Vyukov, *AddressSanitizer: A Fast Address Sanity Checker*, USENIX ATC 2012 — https://www.usenix.org/system/files/conference/atc12/atc12-final39.pdf （"73% slowdown and 3.4x increased memory usage"、"over 300 previously unknown bugs in the Chromium browser"、三类 false negative、插桩放在优化流程末端）
12. Song 等, *SoK: Sanitizing for Security*, IEEE S&P 2019, arXiv:1806.04355 — https://arxiv.org/abs/1806.04355 ；https://oaklandsok.github.io/papers/song2019.pdf （"ASan is used in 24 and 19 of the most popular C and C++ projects on GitHub respectively"）
13. Google Sanitizers Wiki, *AddressSanitizerPerformanceNumbers*（SPEC CPU 2006 逐项表，平均 1.93× / 中位数 1.86×；旧版全插桩 1.73×、仅写 1.26×） — https://github.com/google/sanitizers/wiki/AddressSanitizerPerformanceNumbers
14. Google Sanitizers Wiki, MemorySanitizer — https://github.com/google/sanitizers/wiki/MemorySanitizer
15. LWN.net, *Race detection and more with ThreadSanitizer 2*（TSan v1 减速 20–300×、在 Chromium 找到近 180 个 bug） — https://lwn.net/Articles/598212
16. *Bypassing AddressSanitizer*（ASan 绕过手法） — https://dl.packetstormsecurity.net/papers/general/BreakingAddressSanitizer.pdf

**第三方实测/对照（一手实测但非官方）**
17. 现代 C++ 教程 Vol6, *Valgrind 与 ASan 对照：JIT 解释 vs 编译期插桩*（ASan 静默漏报实测 `uninit=-1094795586`；两工具行号对齐 `:20/:19/:17`；leak 分类 2 档 vs 4 档） — https://awesome-embedded-learning-studio.github.io/Tutorial_AwesomeModernCPP/vol6-performance/ch00-performance-mindset/04-memory-safety-asan-valgrind
18. Kavin Chan, *Sanitizer 内部实现深度 · ASan / TSan / MSan / HWASan*（shadow 值编码 `0xFA`–`0xFF`、redzone 128B、quarantine 默认 256MB、互斥矩阵、开销对比表） — https://kavinchan13.github.io/tech-notes/cpp/sanitizer_internals.html
19. 本项目自有 UB 反例库（环境声明："本机无法链接并运行 ASan/UBSan 运行时"、15 例、伪共享 6.1× 退化 4237 vs 698 ms） — https://liaoranran.github.io/CPP-Bible/Appendix/ub/
20. 本机实测（GCC 15.3.0 / GCC 13.1.0 / Clang 22.1.8，2026-09-29）：sanitizer 运行时链接失败原始错误串，见 `_tmp/exp/`（工作副本，不进入仓库）

Microsoft Learn, `/fp（指定浮点行为）`（"compiler doesn't perform algebraic transformations ... such as reassociation"） — https://learn.microsoft.com/zh-cn/cpp/build/reference/fp-specify-floating-point-behavior?view=msvc-170 ；英文版 https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/cpp/build/reference/fp-specify-floating-point-behavior?view=msvc-170

### 74_失眠内耗怎么应对
1. WHO：Burn-out an "occupational phenomenon"（ICD-11 三维度定义）— https://www.who.int/standards/classifications/frequently-asked-questions/burn-out-an-occupational-phenomenon
2. Ruan JY, Liu Q, Chung KF, Ho KY, Yeung WF. Effects of sleep hygiene education for insomnia: A systematic review and meta-analysis. *Sleep Medicine Reviews*, 2025, Vol 82, Article 102109（42 RCT / 4,245 人）— https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S1087079225000620
3. Hwang JW, Lee GE, Woo JH, Kim SM, Kwon JY. Systematic review and meta-analysis on fully automated digital cognitive behavioral therapy for insomnia. *npj Digital Medicine*, 2025（29 RCT / 9,475 人）— https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC11903857/
4. 同上，Nature 官方 PDF — https://www.nature.com/articles/s41746-025-01514-4.pdf
